Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cognitive operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 974. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 432 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by cognitive generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 974, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 432. QED.
